CURRICULUM 
MHSAA Membership Resolution 
s c h o o l s  w h i c h  a r e  u n d e r  t h e  d i r e c t i o n  o f  t h i s  B o a r d  o f  
Education/Governing Body of the Troy School District, City of 
Troy,  County  of  Oakland,  of  the  state  of  Michigan,  are 
hereby: 
(A)  enrolled  as  members  of  the  Michigan  High  School 
Athletic Association, Inc., a nonprofit association, and 
(B)  are  further  enrolled  to  participate  in  the  approved 
interschool athletic  
  activities sponsored by said association. 
The Board of Education/Governing Body hereby delegates to 
the  Superintendent  or  his/her  designee(s)  the  responsibility 
for  the  supervision  and  control  of  said  activities  and  hereby 
accepts the Constitution and By‐Laws of said association and 
adopts  as  its  own  the  rules,  regulations  and  interpretations 
(as  minimum  standards)  as  published  in  the  current 
HANDBOOK  as  the  governing  code  under  which  the  said 
school(s) shall conduct its program of interscholastic athletics 
and agrees to primary enforcement of said rules, regulations, 
interpretations  and  qualifications.    In  addition,  it  is  hereby 
agreed  that  schools  which  host  or  participate  in  the 
association’s  meets  and  to urnaments  shall  follow  and 
enforce all tournament policies, procedures and schedules. 
This authorization shall be effective from August 1, 2017, and 
shall  remain  effective  until  July  31,  2018,  during  which  the 
authorization may not be revoked.

7. CURRICULUM 
A. CONSIDERATION:   Schools of Choice - Section 105c 
     9th Grade for 2018 – 2019  
In accordance with Michigan  state law, school districts  shall pass a resolution 
each school year if they intend to participate in a Schools of Choice program.  It 
is anticipated that seats will be available for the 2018-2019 school year for grade 
9 at the International Academy East.  It would be appropriate at this time to 
consider adopting a Schools of Choice section 105c resolution for 90 non-resident 
9th grade students  who reside outside of Oakland County , if that intermediate 
school district is contiguous with the Oakland Intermediate School District 
boundaries.   
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Adopt the following resolution. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by: __________________________ 
 Supported by: __________________________ 
Be it therefore resolved that the Board of Education of the Troy School 
District, exercising the option permitted by Section 105c  of the State School Aid 
Act of 1979, as amended by Public Act 300 of 1996, will accept applications for a 
Schools of Choice program for the 2018-2019 school year for 90 non-resident 9th 
grade students who reside outside of Oakland  County, if that Intermediate 
School District is contiguous w ith the Oakland Intermediate School District 
boundaries for the purposes of attending the International Academy East 
program located in the Troy School District. 
Yes: __________ 
No: __________

8. BUSINESS 
A.  CONSIDERATION:  Video Surveillance Software System 
Upgrade 
The Troy School District collaborates with the City of Troy for its video 
surveillance system.  The City needs to update its system which also 
requires the District to update its software as well.  It is recommended 
the bid be awarded as stated below.  
 RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Accept the resolution below. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by:   
 Supported by: 
 Be it therefore resolved that the video surveillance software upgrade be 
awarded to Tyco SimplexGrinnell in the amount of $44,682.00 and that this 
amount be approved as an expenditure of the of the General Fund. 
 Yes: 
 No: 
147
To:   Board of Education 
From:   Beth Soggs 
Date:   July 26, 2017 
Subject:  Video Surveillance System Software Upgrade 
As you are aware, the District collaborates with the City of Troy for its video surveillance system.  The 
City needs to update its system to Pelco’s latest VideoXpert software platform, and as a result the 
District also needs to update its software.  The current Pelco software that is utilized by the City and 
District has been discontinued by Pelco, and an end of support announcement is expected in the future.  
The City wants to ensure that it remains on a supported platform and desires several of the features 
available in the new software.  
At the time of the City upgrade, the District‘s system will be converted to a standalone Pelco VideoXpert 
Enterprise system.  The City will repurpose some of its existing hardware to provide the District the 
hardware necessary for this configuration and software update.  After the update, the District will 
manage its own system on the updated platform, with the City retaining access to view District cameras.  
There are several benefits to the District as part of this update which improve access to footage at the 
building level and provide better search capabilities.  With the current configuration, footage can only 
be accessed from a few specific computers in the District, and access to footage is slow due to the 
connection speed between City and District.  The updated software and configuration allows the Pelco 
client to be installed on computers anywhere on the District network, which provides greater access to 
Safe-Ed and building administration. 
The cost for the District portion of this project is $44,682, for the software, licensing, and associated 
installation services.  There are no additional annual license fees, and all current hardware is fully 
supported with the new software.  The joint proposal for this update is attached for your review. 
Please contact me with any questions.

Introduction 
We are pleased to submit the following cost proposal to upgrade the current Video Surveillance 
Systems (VSS) for the City of Troy and Troy School District, in Troy MI.  
SimplexGrinnell has been providing security and life safety solutions to the City of Troy over a 
decade.  Our experience and cost competitive solutions along with our customized customer 
service will ensure that the City of Troy and Troy School District receives the high quality of 
support for the facilities.  
Enclosed please find the following scope of work, clarifications and cost breakdowns describing 
our approach to successfully completing the above referenced project. 
Statement of Work 
This proposal has been developed from our discussions, site evaluation, and understanding of 
the City of Troy and Troy School District Video Surveillance System and its infrastructure. All 
purposed VSS upgrades are design‐build, and based upon system requirements set forth and 
approved by City of Troy and Troy School District. 
All proposed equipment is consistent with the City of Troy Integrated Security Management 
System and its architecture.  Material prices and labor rates adhere to City of Troy & Troy 
School District 2017 Material & Labor, Blanket Agreement. 
Safer. Smarter. Tyco.™ 
149
Scope of Work 
The following paragraphs are intended to summarize our portion of the work, which is by no 
means all encompassing.  Our proposal includes PELCO professional on‐site services, 
programming, installation, configuration, project coordination, start‐up, training and warranty 
support to furnish a complete operational system.  
City of Troy
The City of Troy’s video surveillance system will be upgraded to PELCO’s latest VideoXpert (VX) 
Ultimate version. VX Ultimate allows the City of Troy to view cameras on another system, in 
this case, the Troy School District system which will be separated under this plan. 
The City of Troy will repurpose the existing System Managers SM5200 Servers by retaining one 
as a DHCP and Network Time Server. The other unit will be provided to Troy School District to 
act in the same role. 
The City of Troy’s 911 Communication Center “video wall” will be converted to an Ops Center 
Workstation, capable of driving the 4 existing monitors. Any installation of cables if required 
will be quoted separately. 
All existing Video Control Display (VCDs) monitoring workstations (except above) will remain 
and will function as is while the SM5200 is still present. Any new cameras will be added to the 
transition these locations over to the license free ‘Ops Center’ software as required (Owner 
furnished PC workstation will be required for this option).  
(if band width is adequate) through the web portal. 
The system will be upgraded over four days which will result in disruption and the potential for 
lost video during that time. 
All existing WS5070’s or above (5080s etc.) are suitable to have the license free ‘Ops Center’

upgrade.
